1. **R1 guardrail plus global hook guard** (`rust/tcl-compiler/src/command_binding.rs`). Coordinator rulings of 11:20Z and 12:50Z.
   - `command_world_closed_on_normal_return(target, facts)`. A handler without a closed receipt keeps the completed world only when it:
     - resolves to an unperturbed registry `Builtin` with no prefix;
     - has complete argument roles and a selected subcommand;
     - has no command-table mutation;
     - if it declares transitions: the list is empty and there is no re-entrant callback;
     - if it declares none (`puts`): its traits carry no `extension_default::{EVALUATION, TRACES}`.
   - `requires_reentry_guard(facts)` now replaces the old capability-as-registration check: same-invocation SourceCallbackRetention must be Absent, precise prefix/host effects remain guarded, and selected Trace::Add/Namespace::SetUnknown plus the variable-trace trait retain the global obligation. UNKNOWN capability is not converted into an absence receipt; generic handler refusal remains independent.
   - `ModuleCommandBindings::unknown_handler_is_original()`: the binding must not be unknown, and every `::unknown` target must be registry-backed and not source-allocated. The prior rule was relaxed after g3; pre-fix g4 still failed the ten positive subcases above. g3's stricter rule (kind == Builtin) withheld the world for all positive cases, so the stock `::unknown` is not kind Builtin. The probe `ZZ_UNKNOWN_HANDLER` prints its targets.

At top level (Direct mode) `p` resolves (see `compiler-names/g3-zz-probe.txt`: `ZZ_SELECT_TARGET … import … registered=Some(Unknown)` versus `export` → NamedInvocation).
